- **ooda**: Routes any decision, action, or iteration through John Boyd's real OODA loop — Observe (measure, don't assume), Orient (the schwerpunkt: reframe against stale models and bias — the highest-leverage step), Decide (pick one lever as a hypothesis), Act (ship it as a test, re-observe), loop fast. Fires a conditional UserPromptSubmit nudge on decide/act/iterate/plan/compete language, primed at SessionStart. Boyd-faithful: not a 4-box speed circle — Orient dominates, and tempo means unpredictable rhythm that outpaces the problem, not raw haste.
